- **graphify**: Standalone graphify knowledge-graph runner with Obsidian integration. - **gwsadm-mcp**: Google Workspace security-audit MCP for Claude Code, through a service account with domain-wide delegation. Investigate suspicious/disabled logins, suspended accounts, third-party OAuth grants, Drive external sharing, group delivery policy, and Gmail message tracing across one or more Workspace domains, plus a one-shot daily_brief. Read-only — no Directory/Reports/Gmail write calls anywhere in this package. Needs a local GWSADM_CONFIG INI file, and the Google Cloud service-account JSON key(s) it references, already present on the machine; the plugin cannot create or supply those files for you.
